### 10. user (2026-06-21T13:29:59.354Z)

1	# PEP 621 packaging for the badminton/sports highlight indexer (DEFERRED #7).
2	#
3	# Build backend: hatchling. `backend/` is an intentional NAMESPACE package
4	# (no `backend/__init__.py`; mypy.ini sets explicit_package_bases for the same
5	# reason), so the wheel target packages are listed EXPLICITLY below — hatchling's
6	# auto-detection would otherwise miss the un-__init__'d `backend` root.
7	#
8	# torch/torchvision are deliberately NOT listed in [project.dependencies]:
9	# their CPU/CUDA wheels need an out-of-band index (--index-url / --extra-index-url),
10	# which PEP 621 cannot express. Install them separately — see [project.optional-dependencies]
11	# `gpu` (documentation only) and the README. CI installs CPU torch on its own line.
12	[build-system]
13	requires = ["hatchling"]
14	build-backend = "hatchling.build"
15	
16	[project]
17	name = "badminton-highlight-indexer"
18	version = "0.1.0"
19	description = "AI-assisted rally segmentation and highlight indexing for racket sports."
20	readme = "README.md"
21	requires-python = ">=3.10"
22	license = { text = "MIT" }
23	authors = [{ name = "Avi Dullu", email = "avi.dullu@gmail.com" }]
24	keywords = ["badminton", "sports", "video", "highlights", "rally", "segmentation"]
25	
26	# Runtime deps mirror requirements.txt, EXCEPT torch/torchvision (see header).
27	dependencies = [
28	    "fastapi>=0.111.0",
29	    "uvicorn>=0.30.1",
30	    "opencv-python>=4.9.0.80",
31	    "numpy>=1.26.4",
32	    "pydantic>=2.7.1",
33	    "jinja2>=3.1.4",
34	    "python-dotenv>=1.0.0",
35	    "google-genai>=2.4.0",
36	    "supervision>=0.21.0,<0.30.0",
37	]
38	
39	[project.optional-dependencies]
40	# Test + lint/type tooling — matches the CI lanes (.github/workflows/ci.yml).
41	dev = [
42	    "pytest",
43	    "pytest-cov",
44	    "httpx",        # fastapi TestClient transport
45	    "ruff",
46	    "mypy",
47	    "PyJWT[crypto]>=2.8",  # M9 edge-auth: Cf-Access JWT verify (RS256) — tests + CI
48	]
49	
50	# M9 edge-auth (Cloudflare Access). DEFAULT-OFF: only needed when security.edge_auth_enabled=True.
51	# `jwt` is lazy-imported, so the base runtime works without this; install it for hosted/multi-tenant.
52	auth = ["PyJWT[crypto]>=2.8"]
53	
54	# Per-video observability reports (SOFT dependency: the pipeline degrades to a
55	# no-op if absent). Pinned to its git tag per BACKLOG. The private repo is
56	# fetched over SSH; if you lack access, simply omit this group — reporting
57	# tests skip via pytest.importorskip. For local dev against a sibling checkout:
58	#   pip install -e ../sports-obsreport --no-build-isolation
59	reporting = [
60	    "obsreport @ git+ssh://git@github.com/avidullu/sports-obsreport.git@v0.1.3",
61	]
62	
63	# `gpu` is DOCUMENTATION ONLY — it is intentionally empty. torch/torchvision
64	# CUDA wheels require `--index-url`/`--extra-index-url`, which PEP 621 cannot
65	# express, so they are NOT declarable here. Install them out-of-band instead:
66	#   # CUDA 12.4 box:
67	#   pip install torch>=2.12.0 torchvision>=0.27.0 --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu124
68	#   # CPU-only box (also what CI uses):
69	#   pip install torch torchvision --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu
70	gpu = []
71	
72	# Storage backends (docs/DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/). The default `local` backend needs NONE of
73	# these. Cloud SDKs are imported lazily, so `import backend.storage` works without them;
74	# install the extra for the bucket you use. (boto3's S3 client covers AWS + Cloudflare R2 +
75	# DO Spaces + MinIO via endpoint_url.) `gdrive://` needs NO extra — it reads a locally
76	# MOUNTED Google Drive (Google Drive for Desktop) as plain files; see backend/storage/gdrive.py.
77	storage-s3 = ["boto3>=1.34"]
78	storage-gcs = ["google-cloud-storage>=2.16"]
79	
80	[project.scripts]
81	# `indexer --server`, `indexer --help`, etc. -> backend/main.py::main()
82	indexer = "backend.main:main"
83	
84	[tool.hatch.metadata]
85	# The `reporting` extra pins obsreport to a git tag (a PEP 508 *direct
86	# reference*); hatchling requires opting in to allow those in metadata.
87	allow-direct-references = true
88	
89	[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel]
90	# `backend` is a namespace package (no __init__.py); list both import roots
91	# explicitly so an editable/wheel install exposes `import backend` and
92	# `import training` regardless of cwd.
93	packages = ["backend", "training"]
94

<result>{"understanding":[{"area":"App launch + current packaging surface (how a user goes from nothing to a running server+worker; what `pip install .` provides; entrypoints; config/DB/output resolution; server-vs-worker process model)","summary":"Today the engine is NOT a downloadable batteries-included app — it is a source checkout you `pip install -e .` from. The PEP 621 build (hatchling, `pyproject.toml:12-93`) installs the `backend` + `training` namespace packages and registers ONE console script, `indexer = backend.main:main` (`pyproject.toml:80-82`), but deliberately omits torch/torchvision (their CUDA/CPU wheels need `--index-url`, which PEP 621 can't express), so `pip install .` alone yields a torch-less install that cannot run the GPU/yolo_hybrid/trajectory paths. ffmpeg/ffprobe are unmanaged system deps. The intended cold-start path is: (1) install ffmpeg, (2) `pip install -e .` (or `.[dev]`), (3) install torch out-of-band, (4) run `scripts/doctor.py` / `indexer --setup` to GPU-probe + write a default `config.json` + `pip install -r requirements.txt` --user, (5) set `GEMINI_API_KEY` in a `.env`, (6) launch. The launcher resolves config.json, .env, the SQLite DB, and output/ all CWD-RELATIVE — there is no app-data dir, no XDG/AppData home, no env override for their location — so the running directory IS the implicit state root. ### 23. user (2026-06-21T14:12:29.737Z)

================ SYNTHESIS ================

--- EXECUTIVE SUMMARY ---
 Ship the engine as ONE pip-installable artifact whose behavior is selected entirely by the already-merged decoupled-compute seam (deployment.profile / compute_target / StorageBackend / ComputeBackend), fronted by a thin friendly launcher and a Windows installer — NOT a frozen monolith and NOT an Electron/Tauri shell. The single hardest constraint forces the scope: torch is deliberately out of pyproject (needs --index-url), and native WASB requires a SECOND, incompatible torch env (conda py3.8 + torch 1.11+cu113) shelled out via $WASB_PYTHON, so no single venv/freeze can deliver the GPU-quality path. The honest, shippable v1 is therefore a LIGHT tier (Gemini cloud-brain + motion + cpu-mock stub) that needs zero torch, zero GPU, and preserves CUJ-1..5 and CUJ-8; the native-WASB GPU path (CUJ-6/7) ships ONLY to the owner / a separately-provisioned env until two blockers clear: WASB-C3 weight provenance (gate_verdict in training/gen0/harness.py:177 hardcodes ship=False) and a reproducible truly-local native config. Five engine prerequisites must land BEFORE any packaging push, each as a small default-OFF PR: (1) a resolved APP-DATA HOME threaded through config/.env/DB/output/static (today everything is CWD-relative — loader.py:30 reads Path('config.json') from CWD while scripts/doctor.py:57 writes it to REPO_ROOT, a silent data-loss footgun); (2) redact GET /api/config which today returns the whole AppConfig object unauthenticated (backend/main.py:144-146); (3) make the bind address and the shipped default profile config-driven (uvicorn hardcodes host='127.0.0.1' at main.py:756); (4) declare scipy (serving-required via backend/features/rally_seq.py, only transitive today) and add a license-scan + built-artifact CI lane; (5) build the three net-new endpoints the UI/wizard depend on (/api/capabilities, /api/videos list, /api/reels) — confirmed absent. Distribution leads with a generated install script over uv (auto-installs Python + the right torch index) plus an Inno Setup Windows installer; a Docker image (the existing deploy/cloudrun/Dockerfile, micromamba-ified) serves the cloud/server profile and the BYO-GPU path. All cloud, edge-auth, cost-ledger, and training features stay default-OFF so the local single-user app is byte-identical to the verified CUJs.

--- RECOMMENDED ARCHITECTURE ---
 ONE build, profile-selected. Ship a single pip wheel (the existing hatchling build: console script `indexer = backend.main:main`, packages=[backend, training]) plus the cross-repo SPA built into it as package data. The pure DETECT->WINDOW->STITCH core never branches on profile (design D1); a single `deployment.profile` switch (backend/config/presets.py PROFILE_PRESETS: truly-local / cloud-serving / cpu-mock) applies a coherent preset bundle, and `get_compute_backend(config)` (backend/compute/__init__.py) returns CloudRunCompute only for compute_target=='cloud_run' else None=in-process. This is the seam to BUILD ON, not reinvent — local vs cloud is genuinely one config flip, and the dispatched Cloud Run Job runs the SAME image forced inline. The app defaults to the truly-local preset (skip_ai_handoff handled per tier) so the out-of-box experience is zero-cloud unless the user opts in.

HOW ONE BUILD RUNS LOCAL + CLOUD: the local FastAPI server (with its in-process ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) job worker, backend/api/job_worker.py:37) is the control plane in every profile. truly-local runs detect/stitch in-process. cloud-serving flips compute_target=cloud_run so the local control plane dispatches a Cloud Run GPU Job and bucket-polls a done-marker; storage.backend=gcs routes I/O. cpu-mock gives a $0 deterministic self-test on first run. The user toggles profile in Settings (rendered from a NEW /api/capabilities endpoint) with explicit confirm (D15 — cloud spend never silent) and an INR cost estimate from backend/billing.py before any cloud job.

HOW THE UI IS BUNDLED: build khelsutra/web (`npm ci && npm run build`) at package time and vendor web/dist/ into the wheel via a hatchling force-include mapping to a package dir (e.g. backend/ui/), served by FastAPI with an SPA history-fallback route. The SPA already calls same-origin `const BASE='/api'` (client.ts:7) so ZERO SPA code change is needed for single-origin serving — this collapses the appliance's reverse-proxy topology into one FastAPI process. Retire the legacy CWD-relative backend/api/static mount (main.py:71-72) and replace the hardcoded relative paths with importlib.resources package-relative resolution. dist/ is gitignored, so the build must produce it and record the bundle hash + an /api/version handshake + a contract test pinning SPA<->engine.

HOW THE DUAL-TORCH / WEIGHTS / FFMPEG PROBLEMS ARE SOLVED: (a) DUAL-TORCH — do NOT try to host both ABIs in one venv or freeze. v1 LIGHT tier carries no torch at all (Gemini/motion/stub). The GPU/WASB HEAVY tier is a SEPARATELY provisioned second environment: short-term via a micromamba+conda-forge env (license-clean, sidesteps the Anaconda-defaults ToS the current deploy/cloudrun/Dockerfile:35 still trips) reached by $WASB_PYTHON exactly as native_wasb_runner.py:89 already does; the strategically correct long-term fix is to EXPORT WASB to ONNX (the DetectorRunner ABC already names an ONNXRunner writing the identical Frame,Visibility,X,Y CSV) so the WASB path needs neither torch 1.11 nor 2.x — onnxruntime (MIT, ~tens of MB, CPU+CUDA+DirectML+CoreML) collapses the dual-env entirely and is owner-gated on a byte-parity re-validation. (b) WEIGHTS — never bake wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar into the artifact (size + WASB-C3 provenance UNRESOLVED). Fetch on first run with a pinned SHA-256, resumable, into the app-data cache, setting $WASB_WEIGHTS. CRITICAL: until counsel clears C3, the public download must NOT auto-fetch these weights — the WEIGHTS path is owner-only in v1. (c) FFMPEG — do NOT redistribute ffmpeg (the current encoder is GPL libx264 at compiler.py:253 and video.py:42). Route every ffmpeg/ffprobe call through ONE resolver helper (config path / env / shutil.which / fail-loud), and have first-run setup (extending scripts/doctor.py, which already prints the winget command) detect-and-offer `winget install Gyan.FFmpeg`. Keep an LGPL-ffmpeg-bundle (BtbN, openh264/NVENC) as a documented fallback that also requires swapping the libx264 default — tracked, not v1.

TIERS: LIGHT (v1, default) — pip/uv install or Windows installer, no torch, Gemini/motion/cpu-mock, full reel CUJ on any CPU box. HEAVY/local-GPU (owner + power user) — adds the micromamba WASB env + weight fetch, gated on C3 + reproducible config + an ONNX milestone. CLOUD/server — the Docker image for a GCP/Cloud Run or BYO-GPU-VM deploy behind Cloudflare Access edge-auth.

--- DISTRIBUTION TIERS ---

• LIGHT (v1 default, recommended) | audience: Non-tech India coach/parent on Windows; any CPU-only machine; the broad beachhead
  contents: pip wheel (indexer console script) + bundled SPA + Gemini(google-genai, hard dep)/motion/cpu-mock segmenters. NO torch, NO GPU, NO WASB weights. ffmpeg resolved via winget-offer at first run. Defaults to truly-local profile; Gemini needs a user-supplied key (consent copy that <=1280px chunks leave the machine, never a training source).
  install_ux: Inno Setup .exe (Start-Menu shortcut -> friendly launcher -> auto-opens 127.0.0.1:8000) as the primary non-tech path; a generated install script over uv (auto-downloads Python 3.10+, isolates the venv, installs the app) as the cross-platform/power-user path. First run: cpu-mock self-test proves the install, then a 3-step in-UI wizard (key/compute/videos).

• HEAVY / local-GPU (owner + opt-in power user) | audience: Owner with the golden corpus + a CUDA box; later, GPU-equipped power users once blockers clear
  contents: LIGHT + a SEPARATELY provisioned WASB env (micromamba+conda-forge, py3.8/torch1.11+cu113) reached via $WASB_PYTHON; main-env torch installed out-of-band via uv --torch-backend=auto; WASB weights fetched from GCS (SHA-256-pinned) at first run. GATED on WASB-C3 counsel sign-off + a reproducible native config; the ONNX-export milestone supersedes this env when it lands.
  install_ux: An opt-in 'Enable local GPU detection' step in the launcher/installer that provisions the second env with a loud resumable progress UI (multi-GB torch wheels on Indian bandwidth) and a post-install torch.cuda.is_available() validation; falls back to LIGHT with a 'GPU not available, running cloud/CPU' banner.

• CLOUD / server | audience: GCP deploy (Cloud Run GPU Jobs, per ADR-013) and BYO-GPU-VM (deploy/gcp/create_gpu_vm.sh); the hosted-service or self-host-on-a-box operator
  contents: The deploy/cloudrun/Dockerfile worker image (Miniconda -> micromamba+conda-forge to clear the Anaconda ToS), bundling ffmpeg + the dual-env stack; runs cloud-serving (compute_target=cloud_run, storage=gcs) behind Cloudflare Access. Closes the per-video M8 cost ledger when worker usage-emission is wired.
  install_ux: `gcloud run jobs create` per deploy/cloudrun/README.md, or create_gpu_vm.sh/teardown.sh for a warm box. A secondary 'Deploy to Render' README button is CPU-only control-plane only (Render/Railway have NO GPU; Fly GPUs deprecated) and must dispatch GPU work back to GCP. Server bind must become 0.0.0.0 (config-gated) + edge-auth mandatory.

  options: ['PyPI wheel + pip', 'pipx', 'uv tool install + generated script', 'Inno Setup installer', 'PyInstaller frozen onefile']
  REC: Inno Setup installer (non-tech) backed by a uv-based generated script (power/cross-platform); reject PyInstaller
  why: uv is the only channel that auto-installs Python + routes the torch --index-url; an Inno shortcut is the non-scary 'open the app' affordance. ▸ Dual-torch WASB delivery
  options: ['Both torches in one venv (impossible)', 'conda-pack relocatable tarball', 'micromamba+conda-forge env on first run', 'ONNX export (no torch on WASB path)', 'Docker-only']
  REC: micromamba+conda-forge now as the bridge; ONNX export as the strategic fix (owner-gated re-validation)
  why: Two incompatible ABIs cannot share a venv or a freeze. micromamba is BSD and sidesteps the Anaconda-defaults ToS the current Dockerfile:35 still trips. ONNX (onnxruntime MIT) dissolves the dual-env, ships tens-of-MB not 5GB, and gives CPU+CUDA+DirectML+CoreML — but needs a byte-parity gate on a real GPU (owner-side, offline CI can't prove numbers).

▸ ffmpeg delivery
  options: ['System winget install + resolver', 'Bundle imageio-ffmpeg', 'Redistribute LGPL static build']
  REC: Winget-install + one resolver helper; LGPL-bundle as documented fallback
  why: Not redistributing ffmpeg sidesteps the GPL-vs-LGPL landmine entirely (current encoder is GPL libx264). imageio-ffmpeg lacks ffprobe (which the engine requires) and its binary is effectively GPL. An LGPL bundle would force swapping libx264 -> openh264/NVENC at BOTH sites (compiler.py:253, video.py:42).

▸ Launch UX wrapper
  options: ['Bare .bat + webbrowser.open', 'pystray system-tray launcher', 'Electron/Tauri embedded-webview shell']
  REC: Friendly launcher that health-polls then opens the browser; REJECT pystray (LGPL-3.0) for an MIT tray lib or stdlib-only; reject Electron/Tauri
  why: D10 locks 'self-hosted webserver, NOT a desktop app'. A launcher that opens the user's own browser at 127.0.0.1:8000 honors that; an embedded-webview shell IS a desktop app and adds WebView2/Chromium friction + presumes a PyInstaller freeze. pystray is LGPL-3.0 — forbidden by the guardrail; pick infi.systray (or equivalent MIT) or ship the bare launcher. Get owner re-ratification in writing.

▸ Cloud target
  options: ['Cloud Run GPU Jobs', 'Render/Railway', 'Fly.io GPU', 'BYO GPU VM']
  REC: Cloud Run GPU Jobs (primary, ADR-013) + BYO-GPU-VM (warm path); Render only as CPU control-plane
  why: Render/Railway have no GPU; Fly GPUs are deprecated. Cloud Run L4 is per-second, scale-to-zero, GCP-co-located with the bucket + cost ledger, and the CloudRunCompute dispatch is already built/mock-tested. Render/Railway pull compute off GCP (ADR-013 tension) so only as a CPU front door dispatching back to GCP.

# Batteries-Included App — Packaging & Distribution Plan

> **Status:** `DRAFT` — owner-gated (scope call O2 is load-bearing) · **Owner:** Avi Dullu · **Created:** 2026-06-21 · **Last updated:** 2026-06-21
> **Lifecycle:** `DRAFT → IN PROGRESS → DONE → archived` (move to `docs/archives/` when DONE)
> **Tracking anchors:** §7 progress tracker is the source of truth; index in `docs/README.md`; pointer in memory `current-state` + `docs/NEXT_STEPS.md` once work starts.
> **Relation to existing docs:** **supersedes** the narrative of `docs/DESKTOP_APP_PLAN.md` (per locked D10 — self-hosted webserver, not a desktop app); **builds on** `docs/COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/` (the profile/storage/compute seam) and is the **distribution layer** under `khelsutra/docs/LOCAL_APPLIANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md`.
> **Honesty note:** claims tagged `[verified]` (checked at file:line this session), `[researched]` (web/needs sign-off), `[design]` (proposed). Not legal advice — licensing items need counsel.

---

## 0. TL;DR
Ship the engine as **ONE pip-installable artifact whose behaviour is selected entirely by the already-merged decoupled-compute seam** (`deployment.profile` / `compute_target` / Storage+Compute backends), fronted by a friendly launcher + a Windows installer — **not** a frozen monolith, **not** an Electron/Tauri shell. The hard constraint that forces scope: torch is deliberately out of `pyproject` (needs `--index-url`) **and** native WASB needs a *second, incompatible* torch env (conda py3.8 + torch 1.11+cu113) shelled out via `$WASB_PYTHON` `[verified native_wasb_runner.py:89]` — so no single venv/freeze can deliver the GPU-quality path. **The honest v1 is a LIGHT tier** (Gemini + motion + cpu-mock, *zero* torch/GPU) that preserves CUJ-1..5/8; the native-WASB GPU path (CUJ-6/7) ships **owner-only** until two blockers clear (WASB-C3 weight provenance + a reproducible truly-local config). **Five small engine prerequisites must land before any packaging push** (§7 P0). The single most actionable finding: **all mutable state is CWD-relative today and `--setup`/server disagree on `config.json`** — a silent data-loss footgun that P0 must fix first.

## 1. Problem & goal
**Goal:** a non-technical user (the primary persona: an India coach/parent on Windows — `docs/UI_PROPOSAL.md` §2) **downloads and runs** the indexer **without sourcing the repo**. The app spins up a **local web service** for (a) inference video→rallies→reel, (b) BYO **training**, and (c) **rally annotation** if needed — runnable **locally and against cloud services** — and **preserves every completed CUJ** (§ CUJ map).

**Why now:** scaffolding + the decoupled-compute serving seam (M1–M8) are merged and default-OFF; the SPA + i18n + the dry-run CUJ are done. What's missing is the *distribution* story: today the only path is `git clone` → `pip install -e .` → out-of-band torch → manual ffmpeg → set up the WASB conda env → build the SPA from a *different repo*. This plan collapses that into download-and-run.

**Read to get current:** `pyproject.toml`, `backend/main.py` (`main()` + `--server`), `backend/config/{loader,presets,models}.py`, `backend/storage/*`, `backend/compute/*`, `backend/pipeline/detectors/native_wasb_runner.py`, `deploy/cloudrun/Dockerfile`, `docs/COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/README.md`, `khelsutra/web/` + `khelsutra/docs/LOCAL_APPLIANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md`.

## 2. Decisions locked (do not relitigate)

| # | Decision | Source / date | Implication for packaging |
|---|----------|---------------|---------------------------|
| L1 | **Pure DETECT→WINDOW→STITCH core never branches on profile**; one build, profile-selected | COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING D1 | Local vs cloud is **one config flip**, not a fork — build ON this, don't reinvent. |
| L2 | **Profiles** = truly-local / cloud-serving / cpu-mock via `PROFILE_PRESETS` | M1 #279 `[verified backend/config/presets.py]` | The app exposes a single "where does compute run" choice. |
| L3 | **Compute = GCP only** (Cloud Run GPU Jobs); DigitalOcean dropped | ADR-013, 2026-06-20 | Cloud tier targets Cloud Run + BYO-GPU-VM only. |
| L4 | **Edge-auth required before any non-localhost exposure** (Cloudflare Access + in-app JWT verify) | D9 / M9 `[verified backend/api/auth.py wired default-OFF main.py:294]` | Bind stays `127.0.0.1` by default; `0.0.0.0` only under an edge-auth profile. |
| L5 | **Delivery = self-hosted webserver the user spins up — NOT a desktop app** | D10, 2026-06-20 (supersedes `DESKTOP_APP_PLAN.md`) | Launcher opens the user's *own browser* at localhost; see O1 (re-ratify vs installer/launcher). |
| L6 | **MIT / Apache-2.0 / BSD only** for every dependency — code, data, AND weights | ratified 2026-06-09 | Every *new packaging* dep must clear this (see §3). |
| L7 | **Paid LLMs (Gemini) = inference/serving only, never a training data source**; commercial models trained on paid-LLM labels are ship-blocked | guardrail + `gate_verdict` `[verified harness.py:177 ship=False]` | The wizard's Gemini-key step must say so; training UI must surface the ship-gate. |
| L8 | **User-switchable profiles** ("your machine or our cloud") with explicit confirm — cloud spend never silent | D13 / D15 | "Push to cloud" toggle needs confirm + INR estimate from `backend/billing.py`. |

## 3. Foundation — research / prior art

**Distribution channels `[researched]`** — `uv` is the only channel that can both install a pinned Python *and* route torch's `--index-url`; `pipx`/plain `pip` can't express the torch index. Frozen binaries (PyInstaller/Nuitka) with torch+CUDA are 3–5 GB, can't split CUDA libs, and **cannot host the second WASB env** → rejected for the GPU path. Prior art (Frigate NVR — closest analog: ffmpeg + ML + web + Docker; Ollama; ComfyUI; LM Studio) converges on **two patterns that fit us:** (1) a **container image** for the server/GPU profile, (2) a **first-run download + a friendly launcher** for the local app.

**Licensing landmines `[verified]` (the load-bearing §3 findings):**
- **WASB-C3 is the dominant blocker.** The WASB-SBDT *code* is MIT, but the badminton *checkpoint* provenance is **unresolved** for commercial redistribution — `gate_verdict` hardcodes `ship=False` `[verified training/gen0/harness.py:174-177]`. **Do not conflate MIT-code with cleared-weights.** A public download must **not** auto-fetch the weights until counsel signs off (O3).
- **ffmpeg is GPL via the `libx264` encoder** `[verified backend/pipeline/stitcher/compiler.py:253]`. The license-clean path is **not to redistribute ffmpeg** — resolve a system/winget install instead. `imageio-ffmpeg` also lacks `ffprobe` (which the engine needs) `[researched]`.
- **Anaconda ToS exposure:** the Cloud Run image uses `Miniconda3` + `conda tos accept … pkgs/main`/`pkgs/r` `[verified deploy/cloudrun/Dockerfile:33-37]` — the ≥200-employee ToS trap. Replace with **micromamba + conda-forge** (BSD, no ToS) for the cloud image *and* any local managed WASB env; record an ADR.
- **New packaging deps that clear L6 `[researched]`:** `uv` (Apache/MIT), Inno Setup (permissive), `onnxruntime` (MIT), `micromamba` (BSD-3), Docker/OCI (Apache-2.0), boto3/google-cloud-* (Apache-2.0), stdlib `webbrowser`. **Forbidden:** `pystray` (LGPL-3.0). **Build-time-only:** `Nuitka` 4.x is **AGPL-3.0+** (compiled output has a runtime exception) — usable as a tool, never bundled. `obsreport` (private git dep) stays a truly-optional extra, never bundled.

## 4. Design / architecture

### 4.1 One build, profile-selected (the reuse map)
- **Reuse:** hatchling build + `indexer = backend.main:main` console script `[verified pyproject.toml:80-82]`; `PROFILE_PRESETS` `[verified presets.py]`; `get_compute_backend(config)` returns `CloudRunCompute` only for `compute_target=='cloud_run'` else `None`=in-process `[verified backend/compute/__init__.py]`; `backend/storage/*` (local/gcs/s3/gdrive); the in-process `ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)` job worker `[verified backend/api/job_worker.py]` — **server and worker are the same process for serving** `[verified main.py:729-736]`, so a packaged launcher just runs `indexer --server`.
- **New:** the app defaults to **truly-local**; the user toggles profile in Settings (rendered from a new `/api/capabilities`) with explicit confirm (L8) + INR estimate before any cloud job.

### 4.2 How one build runs local + cloud
The local FastAPI server is the **control plane in every profile.** `truly-local` runs detect/stitch in-process. `cloud-serving` flips `compute_target=cloud_run` → the local control plane dispatches a **Cloud Run GPU Job** (the *same* image, forced inline) and bucket-polls a done-marker; `storage=gcs` routes I/O. `cpu-mock` gives a $0 deterministic self-test on first run.

### 4.3 How the UI is bundled (collapses the cross-repo + reverse-proxy problem)
Build `khelsutra/web` (`npm ci && npm run build`) **at package time** and vendor `web/dist/` into the wheel via a **hatchling force-include** → a package dir (e.g. `backend/ui/`), served by FastAPI with an **SPA history-fallback** route. The SPA already calls **same-origin `BASE='/api'`** `[verified khelsutra/web client.ts:7]` → **zero SPA code change** for single-origin serving. Retire the legacy CWD-relative `backend/api/static` mount `[verified main.py:71-72]`; resolve paths via `importlib.resources`. `dist/` is gitignored → the build must record a **bundle hash** + an `/api/version` handshake + a contract test pinning SPA↔engine.

### 4.4 Dual-torch / weights / ffmpeg — concretely
- **Dual-torch:** do **not** host both ABIs in one venv or freeze. v1 LIGHT carries **no torch at all**. The GPU/WASB HEAVY tier is a **separately-provisioned second env** — short term via **micromamba + conda-forge** reached by `$WASB_PYTHON` exactly as `native_wasb_runner.py:89` already does; **strategic fix = export WASB to ONNX** (the `DetectorRunner` ABC already names an `ONNXRunner` writing the identical `Frame,Visibility,X,Y` CSV) so the WASB path needs *neither* torch 1.11 nor 2.x — `onnxruntime` (MIT, tens of MB, CPU+CUDA+DirectML+CoreML) dissolves the dual-env. Owner-gated on a real-GPU byte-parity re-validation.
- **Weights:** never bake `wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar` into the artifact (size + C3). Fetch on first run, **SHA-256-pinned + resumable**, into the app-data cache, setting `$WASB_WEIGHTS`. **Until C3 clears, the public download must NOT auto-fetch — owner-only flag.**
- **ffmpeg:** route **every** `ffmpeg`/`ffprobe` call through **one resolver** (config path → env → `shutil.which` → fail-loud); first-run setup (extend `scripts/doctor.py`, which already prints the winget command) detects-and-offers `winget install Gyan.FFmpeg`. An LGPL ffmpeg bundle (BtbN, openh264/NVENC) is a documented fallback that also requires swapping the `libx264` default — tracked, not v1.

### 4.5 Tiers
| Tier | Audience | Contents | Install UX |
|------|----------|----------|------------|
| **LIGHT** (v1 default) | Non-tech India coach/parent on Windows; any CPU box | wheel + bundled SPA + Gemini/motion/cpu-mock; **no torch/GPU/weights**; ffmpeg via winget-offer; defaults truly-local; user-supplied Gemini key | **Inno Setup .exe** (Start-Menu → friendly launcher → auto-opens `127.0.0.1:8000`) primary; **uv generated script** cross-platform. First run: cpu-mock self-test → 3-step wizard. |
| **HEAVY / local-GPU** (owner + opt-in power user) | Owner w/ golden corpus + CUDA box | LIGHT + micromamba WASB env (py3.8/torch1.11) via `$WASB_PYTHON`; main torch out-of-band via uv; weights SHA-256-pinned. **Gated on C3 + reproducible config**; ONNX supersedes this env | opt-in "Enable local GPU" step; resumable progress (multi-GB on Indian bandwidth); post-install `torch.cuda.is_available()` validate; falls back to LIGHT. |
| **CLOUD / server** | GCP deploy + BYO-GPU-VM | the `deploy/cloudrun/Dockerfile` image (micromamba-ified); cloud-serving + gcs; behind Cloudflare Access | `gcloud run jobs create` per `deploy/cloudrun/README.md`, or `deploy/gcp` VM scripts. Bind→`0.0.0.0` (config-gated) + edge-auth mandatory. |

## 5. Threat model / risk table (local appliance)
| Asset | Protected? | Residual risk / control |
|-------|-----------|-------------------------|
| Gemini API key | Partially | Today `GET /api/config` returns the **whole `AppConfig` unauthenticated** `[verified main.py:144-146]`. **P0** redacts + adds a serialization test + a Host-header/localhost-origin guard (DNS-rebinding vector against `127.0.0.1`). Key lives in app-home `.env` (or OS keychain), never in config; scrubbed on uninstall. |
| User videos + **human rally labels** | Yes (local) | MD5 video↔label join breaks on re-encode — never re-encode after annotating; add export/backup (P2d). |
| Cloud spend | Yes | Profile flip needs explicit confirm + INR estimate + per-video cap (L8); never silent. |
| SQLite index | Yes | DB migration on upgrade: backup-before-migrate + downgrade guard (P2d). |

## 6. Honest limits — what this does NOT do
- v1 (LIGHT) **does not run native WASB** → it **deliberately drops CUJ-6/7 from the default**. This is the load-bearing scope honesty (O2). Quality on a CPU/Gemini box ≠ the GPU-WASB quality.
- A green offline suite does **not** prove the *built artifact* works (hidden imports, namespace-package resolution with no `backend/__init__.py`, missing data files like fonts) — P0 adds a built-artifact smoke lane.
- "Push to cloud" is **BYO-GCP** today (no hosted-Khelsutra Storage/Compute client exists) — D13 marketing currently overstates a hosted option (O4).
- Training is **not** a closed loop: `worker train` is an offline CLI, `gate_verdict` refuses commercial ship, and in-process jobs don't survive restart `[verified main.py:646 fail_stale_jobs]`. Never advertise annotate→train as done.

## 8. Open questions — owner / external (blocking gates first)
1. **O1 — Re-ratify L5/D10 vs the launcher+installer:** is a Start-Menu shortcut + friendly launcher (opens *your* browser) + Windows installer acceptable as "a self-hosted webserver you spin up", or does any OS-menu affordance cross the "NOT a desktop app" line? **Need it in writing before P2.** (Reject `pystray`/LGPL regardless; stdlib `webbrowser` or an MIT tray lib only.)
2. **O2 — Confirm v1 = LIGHT** (Gemini/motion/cpu-mock, no torch/WASB) and that **CUJ-6/7 (native WASB) is deliberately owner-only** until C3 + reproducible-config clear. *This is the load-bearing scope call.*
3. **O3 — WASB-C3 counsel sign-off:** may the checkpoint be redistributed / auto-fetched into a public build at all? Determines whether a public HEAVY tier is *ever* possible vs WASB-on-cloud-only.
4. **O4 — Cloud in a downloaded app = hosted-Khelsutra (none built) or BYO-GCP (the only thing built)?** Decide before marketing the "push to cloud" toggle (D13 currently overstates hosted).
5. **O5 — Training in v1 UI?** Recommend defer (synth=mock, real=GPU+dual-env, `ship=False`).
6. **O6 — Annotation in v1?** in-browser HTML5 annotator (recommended) vs bundle VLC vs defer.
7. **O7 — Ship AI-drafted kn/hi to real users before native-speaker review, or gate the trilingual UI on review?**
8. **O8 — Shipped DEFAULT `config.json` / profile** = truly-local (so the box never surprise-bills on Gemini nor returns 0 rallies on WASB-without-key)?
9. **O9 — Code-signing budget** (Windows Authenticode now, macOS Developer-ID later) so non-tech users don't hit SmartScreen/AV prompts.

## 9. Definition of done
- [ ] **O1/O2/O8 decided in writing** (scope + delivery shape + default profile).
- [ ] P0a–P0e merged: launching `indexer --server` from **any** CWD finds the same config/DB; `/api/config` leaks nothing; all CI lanes (incl. the new license-scan + built-artifact lanes) green at the coverage floor.
- [ ] P1a/P1b merged: FastAPI serves the **real SPA** single-origin with refresh-survival; capabilities/videos/reels return real data; contract test guards SPA↔engine skew.
- [ ] P2a–P2d merged: a **clean Windows VM → installer → reel downloaded (en+hi)** with **no Python/git/ffmpeg pre-installed**; uninstall is clean; upgrade preserves data.
- [ ] **Every completed CUJ in the map below verified preserved** (CUJ-6/7 explicitly owner-only and flagged, not silently dropped).
- [ ] HEAVY/cloud (P3) + ONNX (P4) tracked but **not gating v1**.

### 37. assistant (2026-06-21T14:16:55.265Z)

Done. I synced, ran an exhaustive 29-agent research workflow, **re-verified every load-bearing claim at `file:line` myself**, and wrote it up as a tracked project doc: **[docs/PACKAGING_PLAN.md](docs/PACKAGING_PLAN.md)** (untracked/uncommitted — I won't open a PR unless you ask). Here's the plan.

## The core idea
**One pip-installable artifact whose behaviour is selected entirely by the decoupled-compute seam you already merged** (`deployment.profile` / `compute_target` / Storage+Compute backends), fronted by a friendly launcher + a Windows installer. **Not** a frozen monolith, **not** an Electron/Tauri shell. Local-vs-cloud is genuinely *one config flip* — you built that seam (M1–M8); this is the **distribution layer** on top, not a rewrite.

## The constraint that forces the shape
Two facts are load-bearing and verified:
1. **torch is deliberately out of `pyproject`** (CUDA/CPU wheels need `--index-url`, which PEP 621 can't express) — `pip install .` gives a torch-less install.
2. **Native WASB needs a *second, incompatible* torch env** (conda py3.8 + torch 1.11+cu113) shelled out via `$WASB_PYTHON` (`native_wasb_runner.py:89`), while the main engine is torch 2.6.

→ **No single venv or freeze can deliver the GPU-quality path.** So the honest v1 is a **LIGHT tier** (Gemini + motion + cpu-mock, *zero* torch/GPU) that preserves CUJ-1..5/8 and runs on any CPU box. Native-WASB (CUJ-6/7) ships **owner-only** until two blockers clear.

## Three tiers
| Tier | Who | Contents | Install |
|---|---|---|---|
| **LIGHT** (v1 default) | Non-tech coach on Windows; any CPU | wheel + bundled SPA + Gemini/motion/cpu-mock; no torch/weights; ffmpeg via winget-offer | **Inno Setup .exe** → launcher → auto-opens `localhost:8000`; uv script for power users |
| **HEAVY/local-GPU** | Owner + opt-in power user | + micromamba WASB env via `$WASB_PYTHON` + SHA-256-pinned weights | opt-in "Enable GPU" step; falls back to LIGHT |
| **CLOUD/server** | GCP / BYO-GPU-VM | the `deploy/cloudrun` image (micromamba-ified) behind Cloudflare Access | `gcloud run jobs create` / VM scripts |

## How the three hard problems get solved
- **Dual-torch:** v1 carries *no* torch. HEAVY uses a separate **micromamba+conda-forge** env (BSD — also fixes the **Anaconda-ToS** trap in `Dockerfile:33-37`). **Strategic fix: export WASB to ONNX** — `onnxruntime` (MIT, tens of MB, CPU+CUDA+DirectML) *dissolves the dual-env entirely* and the `DetectorRunner` ABC already names an `ONNXRunner`.
- **Weights:** never bake them in; fetch on first run, SHA-256-pinned + resumable. **Public download must NOT auto-fetch until counsel clears WASB-C3** (`gate_verdict` already hardcodes `ship=False`).
- **ffmpeg:** don't redistribute (the encoder is GPL `libx264`); one resolver helper + a `winget install Gyan.FFmpeg` first-run offer.
- **SPA-in-another-repo:** build `khelsutra/web` at package time, vendor `web/dist/` into the wheel (hatchling force-include) served single-origin. The SPA already uses same-origin `BASE='/api'` → **zero SPA code change**, and it collapses the appliance reverse-proxy into one FastAPI process.

## Phased plan (small, default-OFF, reversible PRs)
- **P0 — engine prerequisites (Claude-doable, no behaviour change):** ① **app-data HOME** threaded through config/`.env`/DB/output/static (today all CWD-relative, and `doctor.py` writes `config.json` to a *different* place than the server reads — a silent data-loss footgun); ② **redact `/api/config`** (returns the whole `AppConfig` unauthenticated today); ③ config-driven bind/CORS; ④ declare **scipy** (undeclared serving dep) + license-scan + built-artifact CI lanes; ⑤ single-instance lock.
- **P1 — UI bundling + the 3 net-new endpoints** (`/api/capabilities`, `/api/videos` list, `/api/reels`) the wizard needs (confirmed absent) + `/api/version` contract test.
- **P2 — LIGHT installer + first-run wizard** (key/compute/videos, en/kn/hi) + DB backup-before-migrate + uninstall manifest.
- **P3 — HEAVY/cloud (largely owner-gated):** micromamba, weight fetcher (C3-gated), "push to cloud" toggle (confirm + INR estimate), M9 edge-auth.
- **P4 — ONNX export (dissolves dual-env) + training/annotation decisions** (recommend deferring training-in-UI; recommend an in-browser annotator over bundling VLC).

**Every completed CUJ is mapped** in §9 of the doc — CUJ-6/7 (native WASB) is the *one* deliberately absent from the LIGHT default, flagged explicitly rather than silently dropped (the honest version of "preserve every CUJ").

### 49. assistant (2026-06-21T14:22:28.716Z)

Both decisions are now locked into the doc. Here's what changed:

- **L9 (new, locked):** Cross-platform **Windows + macOS + Linux**, **no native component** — the same local webserver powers the UI on all three; distribution is `uv`/pip + a **pure-Python launcher** (`indexer --app` → boot server → open your browser). Electron/Tauri/tray and native-installer-as-runtime are all out. OSes differ *only* in how ffmpeg/torch are acquired (winget / brew / apt).

1	"""Configuration loader and saver.
2	
3	Provides a single place to load validated AppConfig with built-in defaults
4	merged with optional file overrides (config.json).
5	
6	This replaces scattered json.load + .get() patterns throughout the codebase.
7	"""
8	
9	from __future__ import annotations
10	
11	import json
12	import logging
13	import os
14	from pathlib import Path
15	from typing import Any
16	
17	from pydantic import BaseModel
18	
19	from .models import AppConfig
20	from .presets import (
21	    COMPUTE_TARGET_FOR_PROFILE,
22	    PROFILE_PRESETS,
23	    is_known_profile,
24	    preset_for,
25	    suggest_profile,
26	)
27	
28	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
29	
30	DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH = Path("config.json")
31	
32	
33	def _deep_merge(base: dict[str, Any], override: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
34	    """Recursively overlay ``override`` onto ``base`` (override wins; dicts merge, scalars
35	    replace). Used ONLY for the profile-preset precedence path so a config.json sub-key
36	    overrides a preset sub-key while sibling preset/default sub-keys survive — the no-profile
37	    path keeps the original shallow ``{**defaults, **file_data}`` merge unchanged."""
38	    out = dict(base)
39	    for key, val in override.items():
40	        if isinstance(val, dict) and isinstance(out.get(key), dict):
41	            out[key] = _deep_merge(out[key], val)
42	        else:
43	            out[key] = val
44	    return out
45	
46	
47	def _resolve_explicit_profile(file_data: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
48	    """The EXPLICITLY chosen deployment profile, env winning over config.json. ``""`` if none
49	    is set. Auto-detect is deliberately NOT consulted here — it only suggests (D15), so a bare
50	    environment never silently selects a profile (and never silently spends on cloud).
51	
52	    An *unrecognised* ``DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE`` env value warns and FALLS THROUGH to the file's
53	    profile (rather than suppressing it) — so an env typo can't leave the recorded profile
54	    asserting a preset that was never applied. A bad value in config.json is left to surface as
55	    a hard, typed-Literal error downstream (config-file correctness)."""
56	    env_profile = os.environ.get("DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE")
57	    if env_profile and env_profile.strip():
58	        ep = env_profile.strip()
59	        if is_known_profile(ep):
60	            return ep
61	        logger.warning(
62	            "[CONFIG] unrecognized DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE env value %r (valid: %s); ignoring it "
63	            "and falling back to config.json deployment.profile.", ep, ", ".join(PROFILE_PRESETS),
64	        )
65	        # fall through to the file's profile (env typo must not suppress a valid file choice)
66	    dep = file_data.get("deployment")
67	    if isinstance(dep, dict):
68	        prof = dep.get("profile")
69	        if isinstance(prof, str) and prof.strip():
70	            return prof.strip()
71	    return ""
72	
73	
74	def _unknown_key_paths(data: dict[str, Any], model_cls: type[BaseModel], prefix: str = "") -> list[str]:
75	    """Dotted paths in `data` that the typed config will not honor.
76	
77	    Two silent failure modes exist without this check: a typo'd TOP-LEVEL key is
78	    kept by AppConfig's ``extra="allow"`` but never read by typed code, and a
79	    typo'd key INSIDE a section is silently dropped by Pydantic. Either way the
80	    user's intent is lost — collect every such key so load_config can warn.
81	    """
82	    unknown: list[str] = []
83	    fields = model_cls.model_fields
84	    for key, val in data.items():
85	        if key not in fields:
86	            unknown.append(prefix + key)
87	            continue
88	        ann = fields[key].annotation
89	        if isinstance(val, dict) and isinstance(ann, type) and issubclass(ann, BaseModel):
90	            unknown.extend(_unknown_key_paths(val, ann, f"{prefix}{key}."))
91	    return unknown
92	
93	
94	def _get_builtin_defaults() -> dict[str, Any]:
95	    """Return a plain dict of the current built-in defaults.
96	
97	    The AppConfig model is the single source of truth for defaults.
98	    """
99	    return AppConfig().model_dump(mode="json")
100	
101	
102	def load_config(path: str | Path | None = None) -> AppConfig:
103	    """Load configuration with the following precedence:
104	
105	    1. Built-in defaults defined in the Pydantic models.
106	    2. Values from the JSON file (if present and readable).
107	    3. (Future) environment / CLI overrides.
108	
109	    Missing file or unreadable file → returns a fully defaulted AppConfig.
110	    Unknown keys in the file are tolerated (extra="allow" on the model).
111	    """
112	    cfg_path = Path(path) if path is not None else DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH
113	
114	    file_data: dict[str, Any] = {}
115	    if cfg_path.exists():
116	        try:
117	            with open(cfg_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
118	                file_data = json.load(f)
119	        except Exception as exc:
120	            # Non-fatal: continue with defaults + whatever we could parse.
121	            # In production we might log here.
122	            logger.warning(f"Failed to read {cfg_path}: {exc}. Using defaults + partial data.")
123	
124	    # A config.json that is valid JSON but not an OBJECT (e.g. `[]`, `42`, `"x"`, `null`) would
125	    # otherwise crash startup: a truthy non-mapping hits `.items()` in _unknown_key_paths, and any
126	    # non-mapping breaks the `{**defaults, **file_data}` unpack. Degrade to defaults + warn, per the
127	    # loader's "bad input is non-fatal" contract.
128	    if not isinstance(file_data, dict):
129	        logger.warning("%s is not a JSON object (got %s); ignoring it and using defaults.",
130	                       cfg_path, type(file_data).__name__)
131	        file_data = {}
132	
133	    if file_data:
134	        unknown = _unknown_key_paths(file_data, AppConfig)
135	        if unknown:
136	            logger.warning(
137	                "[CONFIG WARNING] %s contains keys the typed config does not "
138	                "recognize (typo'd or removed knobs — top-level extras are kept "
139	                "but unread; unknown section keys are silently dropped): %s",
140	                cfg_path, ", ".join(sorted(unknown)),
141	            )
142	
143	    # Precedence: built-in defaults < profile preset < config.json (< env/--set, applied at
144	    # consumption sites downstream). A *deployment profile* is opt-in — it is applied ONLY when
145	    # explicitly selected (DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE env or config.json deployment.profile). With no
146	    # profile the merge is byte-identical to the pre-M1 loader, so the default path is unchanged.
147	    defaults = _get_builtin_defaults()
148	    profile = _resolve_explicit_profile(file_data)
149	
150	    if profile and not is_known_profile(profile):
151	        # Only an unrecognised value from config.json reaches here (env typos already fell back
152	        # in _resolve_explicit_profile). Warn + apply no preset; the bad value also hits the
153	        # typed Literal at model_validate below, a hard clear error — loud, never silent.
154	        logger.warning(
155	            "[CONFIG] unrecognized deployment profile %r (valid: %s); applying no preset.",
156	            profile, ", ".join(PROFILE_PRESETS),
157	        )
158	        profile = ""
159	
160	    if profile:
161	        merged = _deep_merge(_deep_merge(defaults, preset_for(profile)), file_data)
162	        # Record the profile actually applied — the env may have chosen it over config.json,
163	        # so re-stamp it onto the merged deployment section to keep the record honest.
164	        merged.setdefault("deployment", {})
165	        if isinstance(merged["deployment"], dict):
166	            merged["deployment"]["profile"] = profile
167	            ct = merged["deployment"].get("compute_target")
168	            canonical = COMPUTE_TARGET_FOR_PROFILE.get(profile)
169	            if not ct and canonical:
170	                # An active profile with no explicit compute_target (e.g. config.json blanked it
171	                # to "") gets its canonical target back — the record never silently lies about an
172	                # active profile's compute_target (it stays meaningful for the M2+ ComputeBackend).
173	                merged["deployment"]["compute_target"] = canonical
174	            elif ct and canonical and ct != canonical:
175	                logger.warning(
176	                    "[CONFIG] deployment profile %r normally uses compute_target=%r but "
177	                    "config.json overrides it to %r — honoring the explicit override.",
178	                    profile, canonical, ct,
179	                )
180	            logger.info("[CONFIG] deployment profile ACTIVE: %s (compute_target=%s)",
181	                        profile, merged["deployment"].get("compute_target"))
182	    else:
183	        # No profile → the original shallow merge (Pydantic re-defaults nested sections).
184	        merged = {**defaults, **file_data}
185	        suggestion = suggest_profile()  # cheap, env-only (no torch); SUGGESTS only (D15)
186	        if suggestion:
187	            logger.debug(
188	                "[CONFIG] no deployment.profile set (manual knobs). Environment suggests %r — "
189	                "set deployment.profile or DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE to apply (auto-detect never "
190	                "auto-applies — D15).", suggestion,
191	            )
192	
193	    return AppConfig.model_validate(merged)
194	
195	
196	def save_default_config(path: str | Path | None = None) -> Path:
197	    """Write a fresh default config.json.
198	
199	    Used by scripts/doctor.py diagnostics and the --setup CLI path.
200	    Overwrites if the file exists.
201	    """
202	    cfg_path = Path(path) if path is not None else DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH
203	    cfg = AppConfig()
204	    cfg_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
205	    cfg_path.write_text(cfg.model_dump_json(indent=4), encoding="utf-8")
206	    return cfg_path
207	
208	
209	# Backwards-compatible alias used by some older call sites during transition.
210	def get_default_config() -> dict[str, Any]:
211	    """Return defaults as a plain dict (for transitional use only)."""
212	    return _get_builtin_defaults()

1	"""Path-safety helpers for the API boundary (untrusted request fields)."""
2	import os
3	from typing import Any, Optional
4	
5	
6	def output_base(config: Any) -> str:
7	    """The configured base dir for a segmenter's LOCAL scratch/staging artifacts
8	    (COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING M3). Returns ``output.output_dir`` (default ``"output"``) so the
9	    chunk-staging / AI-handoff / temp dirs follow ``--output-dir`` / ``output.output_dir`` instead of
10	    a hardcoded ``./output``. The DEFAULT is ``"output"`` → byte-identical to the prior literal.
11	
12	    This is the LOCAL output dir, deliberately NOT the storage ``workspace_root`` (which may be a
13	    ``gs://`` URI you can't ``makedirs``). Accepts the typed AppConfig or the legacy dict-shim
14	    (``.get("output", {}).get("output_dir")``); any miss or empty value falls back to ``"output"``."""
15	    try:
16	        out = config.get("output", {}).get("output_dir")
17	    except Exception:
18	        out = None
19	    return out or "output"
20	
21	
22	def safe_output_filename(name: str) -> str:
23	    """Validate an output filename is a BARE name (no dir part, no traversal, not absolute).
24	
25	    `os.path.join(output_dir, name)` silently discards `output_dir` when `name` is absolute,
26	    and `..` segments escape it — so an unchecked `output_filename` is an arbitrary-write
27	    primitive. Returns the name unchanged when safe; raises ValueError otherwise.
28	    """
29	    if not name or not name.strip():
30	        raise ValueError("output_filename must not be empty")
31	    if "\x00" in name:
32	        raise ValueError("output_filename must not contain a null byte")
33	    if "/" in name or "\\" in name:
34	        raise ValueError(f"output_filename must not contain a path separator: {name!r}")
35	    if os.path.isabs(name) or name != os.path.basename(name) or name in (".", ".."):
36	        raise ValueError(f"output_filename must be a bare filename, got: {name!r}")
37	    return name
38	
39	
40	def resolve_under_root(path: str, root: str) -> str:
41	    """Return the real path, asserting it is contained under `root` (raises ValueError if not)."""
42	    rp = os.path.realpath(path)
43	    rr = os.path.realpath(root)
44	    try:
45	        contained = os.path.commonpath([rp, rr]) == rr
46	    except ValueError:  # different drives on Windows
47	        contained = False
48	    if not contained:
49	        raise ValueError(f"path {path!r} escapes the allowed root {root!r}")
50	    return rp
51	
52	
53	def validate_input_path(path: str, allowed_root: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
54	    """Reject null bytes and (when an allowed_root is configured) out-of-root inputs.
55	
56	    With no allowed_root (default, single-user local mode) only the null-byte check applies,
57	    so the local 'point it at any of my videos' workflow is preserved. Setting an
58	    allowed_root (a future hosted/tenant deployment) enforces containment.
59	    """
60	    if "\x00" in path:
61	        raise ValueError("video_path must not contain a null byte")
62	    if allowed_root:
63	        resolve_under_root(path, allowed_root)
64

48	def _cors_origins_from_env(env: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None) -> List[str]:
49	    """M9: CORS allow-origins from the ``RALLY_CORS_ALLOW_ORIGINS`` env var (comma-separated),
50	    default ``["*"]``. Read from the ENV (not a cwd ``config.json``) so importing ``backend.main``
51	    does zero filesystem I/O — a hosted/multi-tenant deploy sets this to lock CORS to its origin."""
52	    src = os.environ if env is None else env
53	    raw = (src.get("RALLY_CORS_ALLOW_ORIGINS", "") or "").strip()
54	    origins = [o.strip() for o in raw.split(",") if o.strip()]
55	    return origins or ["*"]
56	
57	
58	# CORS for the web UI. allow_origins='*' with allow_credentials=True is rejected by browsers per the
59	# fetch spec + is unsafe to carry into multi-tenant; this tool uses no cookies, so credentials stay
60	# off. The origin list is configurable via the env var above (default ["*"]) so a hosted deploy can
61	# lock it to its origin; the middleware is fixed at startup.
62	app.add_middleware(
63	    CORSMiddleware,
64	    allow_origins=_cors_origins_from_env(),
65	    allow_credentials=False,
66	    allow_methods=["*"],
67	    allow_headers=["*"],
68	)

### 75. assistant (2026-06-21T14:32:37.662Z)

"""Resolved application-data HOME for a packaged, batteries-included install.

PACKAGING_PLAN.md P0a. Today every piece of mutable state — ``config.json``, ``.env``,
the SQLite DB, ``output/`` — is resolved RELATIVE TO THE CURRENT WORKING DIRECTORY, and
``scripts/doctor.py`` writes ``config.json`` to the repo root while the server reads it from
the CWD. Launched from an OS app menu (CWD ≠ repo root) those disagree and the user silently
"loses" their index. This module introduces ONE resolver every state path roots on.

Design contract — **DEFAULT IS BYTE-IDENTICAL TO TODAY**:
  * ``RALLY_APP_HOME`` env set  → that directory is the app home (the packaged launcher sets it).
  * ``RALLY_APP_HOME`` unset    → ``Path(".")`` i.e. the CWD, exactly the pre-P0a behaviour.

The OS-default location (``%APPDATA%`` / ``~/Library/Application Support`` / ``~/.local/share``)
is computed by :func:`default_os_app_home` but is **NOT** auto-selected here — the launcher (P2)
calls it and exports ``RALLY_APP_HOME`` explicitly. Keeping the resolver opt-in means importing
this module has zero behavioural effect on the repo/dev/CI/test paths (no surprise writes into a
real user profile during a test run).

stdlib-only (os, platform, pathlib) so it is safe to import at ``backend.main`` line 1 — before
the heavier pydantic-backed ``backend.config`` package — keeping the early ``load_dotenv`` path light.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import platform
from pathlib import Path

#: Environment variable the packaged launcher sets to pin the app-data home.
APP_HOME_ENV = "RALLY_APP_HOME"

#: OS-app-dir leaf used by :func:`default_os_app_home` (the product brand).
APP_DIR_NAME = "Khelsutra"


def app_home() -> Path:
    """The resolved application-data home.

    ``RALLY_APP_HOME`` (``~`` expanded) when set; otherwise ``Path(".")`` — the CWD, byte-identical
    to the pre-P0a behaviour. Does NOT create the directory (callers create on write).
    """
    env = os.environ.get(APP_HOME_ENV)
    if env and env.strip():
        return Path(env.strip()).expanduser()
    return Path(".")


def app_path(*parts: str | os.PathLike[str]) -> Path:
    """A path under :func:`app_home` (e.g. ``app_path("output")``)."""
    return app_home().joinpath(*[str(p) for p in parts])


def default_config_path() -> Path:
    """``<app-home>/config.json`` — the config file the loader reads and ``--setup`` writes.

    With ``RALLY_APP_HOME`` unset this is ``Path("config.json")`` (CWD-relative), unchanged.
    """
    return app_home() / "config.json"


def default_env_path() -> Path:
    """``<app-home>/.env`` — the dotenv file the server prefers (e.g. ``GEMINI_API_KEY``)."""
    return app_home() / ".env"


def resolve_output_dir(output_dir: str) -> str:
    """Root a RELATIVE ``output_dir`` under :func:`app_home`; pass an ABSOLUTE one through.

    With ``RALLY_APP_HOME`` unset, ``resolve_output_dir("output") == "output"`` (CWD-relative),
    byte-identical to today; with it set, ``"output"`` becomes ``<app-home>/output`` so the SQLite
    DB and produced reels live alongside the rest of the app's state.
    """
    if not output_dir:
        return str(app_home() / "output")
    if os.path.isabs(output_dir):
        return output_dir
    return str(app_home() / output_dir)


def default_os_app_home(app_name: str = APP_DIR_NAME) -> Path:
    """The OS-conventional per-user app-data dir (for the packaged LAUNCHER to export as
    ``RALLY_APP_HOME`` — NOT auto-selected by :func:`app_home`).

    * Windows → ``%APPDATA%\\<app_name>`` (``~/AppData/Roaming/<app_name>`` if unset)
    * macOS   → ``~/Library/Application Support/<app_name>``
    * Linux/other → ``$XDG_DATA_HOME/<app_name>`` (``~/.local/share/<app_name>`` if unset)
    """
    system = platform.system()
    if system == "Windows":
        base = os.environ.get("APPDATA") or str(Path.home() / "AppData" / "Roaming")
    elif system == "Darwin":
        base = str(Path.home() / "Library" / "Application Support")
    else:
        base = os.environ.get("XDG_DATA_HOME") or str(Path.home() / ".local" / "share")
    return Path(base) / app_name

### 89. assistant (2026-06-21T14:34:13.662Z)

"""Tests for the resolved application-data HOME (PACKAGING_PLAN.md P0a).

The load-bearing guarantee: with ``RALLY_APP_HOME`` UNSET, every resolver is byte-identical to the
pre-P0a CWD-relative behaviour; with it SET, config/.env/output/DB all root under that one home.
"""

import os
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from backend.utils import app_home as ah
from backend.config import loader


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _clear_app_home_env(monkeypatch):
    """Each test starts with RALLY_APP_HOME unset unless it sets it explicitly."""
    monkeypatch.delenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, raising=False)


# ---- default (env unset) is byte-identical to today ----

def test_app_home_defaults_to_cwd():
    assert ah.app_home() == Path(".")


def test_default_config_path_unset_is_cwd_config_json():
    # Pre-P0a this was the module constant Path("config.json").
    assert ah.default_config_path() == Path("config.json")


def test_default_env_path_unset_is_cwd_dotenv():
    assert ah.default_env_path() == Path(".env")


def test_resolve_output_dir_unset_is_identity_for_relative():
    assert ah.resolve_output_dir("output") == "output"
    assert ah.resolve_output_dir("custom") == "custom"


# ---- RALLY_APP_HOME set → everything roots there ----

def test_app_home_honors_env(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, str(tmp_path))
    assert ah.app_home() == tmp_path


def test_app_home_expands_user(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, "~/khelsutra-data")
    assert ah.app_home() == Path.home() / "khelsutra-data"


def test_app_home_ignores_blank_env(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, "   ")
    assert ah.app_home() == Path(".")


def test_config_and_env_paths_root_under_home(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, str(tmp_path))
    assert ah.default_config_path() == tmp_path / "config.json"
    assert ah.default_env_path() == tmp_path / ".env"
    assert ah.app_path("output", "x.db") == tmp_path / "output" / "x.db"


def test_resolve_output_dir_set(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, str(tmp_path))
    assert ah.resolve_output_dir("output") == str(tmp_path / "output")


def test_resolve_output_dir_absolute_passthrough(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, str(tmp_path))
    abs_dir = str(tmp_path / "elsewhere") if os.path.isabs(str(tmp_path)) else os.path.abspath("elsewhere")
    assert ah.resolve_output_dir(abs_dir) == abs_dir


def test_resolve_output_dir_empty_falls_back_to_home_output(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, str(tmp_path))
    assert ah.resolve_output_dir("") == str(tmp_path / "output")


# ---- default_os_app_home: the OS-conventional dir the launcher exports (NOT auto-selected) ----

def test_default_os_app_home_windows(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setattr(ah.platform, "system", lambda: "Windows")
    monkeypatch.setenv("APPDATA", str(tmp_path / "Roaming"))
    assert ah.default_os_app_home() == tmp_path / "Roaming" / "Khelsutra"


def test_default_os_app_home_macos(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(ah.platform, "system", lambda: "Darwin")
    assert ah.default_os_app_home() == Path.home() / "Library" / "Application Support" / "Khelsutra"


def test_default_os_app_home_linux_xdg(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setattr(ah.platform, "system", lambda: "Linux")
    monkeypatch.setenv("XDG_DATA_HOME", str(tmp_path / "share"))
    assert ah.default_os_app_home() == tmp_path / "share" / "Khelsutra"


def test_default_os_app_home_linux_default(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(ah.platform, "system", lambda: "Linux")
    monkeypatch.delenv("XDG_DATA_HOME", raising=False)
    assert ah.default_os_app_home() == Path.home() / ".local" / "share" / "Khelsutra"


# ---- loader integration: load_config() roots on the app-home ----

def test_loader_reads_config_from_app_home(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, str(tmp_path))
    (tmp_path / "config.json").write_text('{"sport": "tennis"}', encoding="utf-8")
    cfg = loader.load_config()  # no explicit path → default_config_path() under the home
    assert cfg.sport == "tennis"


def test_loader_no_config_in_home_uses_defaults(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, str(tmp_path))  # empty dir, no config.json
    cfg = loader.load_config()
    # Defaults load cleanly (no crash, no file) — same contract as a missing CWD config.json.
    assert cfg is not None
    assert hasattr(cfg, "indexing")


def test_save_default_config_writes_under_app_home(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, str(tmp_path))
    written = loader.save_default_config()  # no explicit path
    assert written == tmp_path / "config.json"
    assert written.exists()


def test_explicit_path_still_overrides_app_home(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, str(tmp_path / "home"))
    explicit = tmp_path / "explicit.json"
    explicit.write_text('{"sport": "squash"}', encoding="utf-8")
    cfg = loader.load_config(str(explicit))
    assert cfg.sport == "squash"

### 127. user (2026-06-21T14:47:46.657Z)

1	"""Regression guard for the `python -m backend.main` double-module job-hang.
2	
3	`python -m backend.main` runs main.py as `__main__`, so `main()` configures `__main__`'s globals
4	(`db_instance`/`global_config`). But `job_worker`/`uploads` resolve shared state via
5	`import backend.main` — a SEPARATE module object whose `db_instance` stays `None`. The async-job
6	drain then crashes silently on `None.claim_due_job()` and every submitted job hangs in `queued`
7	forever. The in-process tests (TestClient + monkeypatched `db_instance` + an inline executor)
8	cannot see this — only the real subprocess does. This spawns the DOCUMENTED invocation and asserts
9	a submitted job actually LEAVES `queued`.
10	"""
11	import json
12	import os
13	import socket
14	import subprocess
15	import sys
16	import time
17	import urllib.error
18	import urllib.request
19	
20	import pytest
21	
22	REPO_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
23	
24	
25	def _free_port() -> int:
26	    s = socket.socket()
27	    s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
28	    port = int(s.getsockname()[1])
29	    s.close()
30	    return port
31	
32	
33	def _get(url: str, timeout: float = 5):
34	    with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=timeout) as r:
35	        return json.loads(r.read().decode())
36	
37	
38	def _post(url: str, payload: dict, timeout: float = 15):
39	    req = urllib.request.Request(
40	        url, data=json.dumps(payload).encode(), headers={"content-type": "application/json"})
41	    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
42	        return json.loads(r.read().decode())
43	
44	
45	def test_python_m_backend_main_drains_submitted_jobs(tmp_path):
46	    """Spawning the engine the documented way (`python -m backend.main --server`) must actually
47	    DRAIN a submitted job — it must leave `queued`, never hang. (A dummy file fails fast; the
48	    verdict is irrelevant — what matters is that the worker claimed and ran it.)"""
49	    port = _free_port()
50	    out_dir = tmp_path / "out"
51	    video = tmp_path / "dummy.mp4"
52	    video.write_bytes(b"not-a-real-video")  # exists -> the drain claims it; processing fails fast
53	    log = open(tmp_path / "server.log", "wb")
54	
55	    proc = subprocess.Popen(
56	        [sys.executable, "-m", "backend.main", "--server", "--port", str(port),
57	         "--output-dir", str(out_dir)],
58	        cwd=REPO_ROOT, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
59	    )
60	    base = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"
61	    try:
62	        deadline = time.time() + 90  # cold import of torch/cv2 can be slow on CI
63	        while time.time() < deadline:
64	            if proc.poll() is not None:
65	                pytest.fail(f"server exited early (code {proc.returncode}); see {tmp_path/'server.log'}")
66	            try:
67	                if _get(f"{base}/api/health").get("status") == "ok":
68	                    break
69	            except (urllib.error.URLError, ConnectionError, OSError):
70	                time.sleep(0.5)
71	        else:
72	            pytest.fail("server never became healthy")
73	
74	        job_id = _post(f"{base}/api/process",
75	                       {"video_path": str(video), "segmenter": "motion"})["job_id"]
76	
77	        status = "queued"
78	        deadline = time.time() + 45
79	        while time.time() < deadline:
80	            status = _get(f"{base}/api/jobs/{job_id}")["status"]
81	            if status != "queued":
82	                break
83	            time.sleep(1)
84	
85	        assert status != "queued", (
86	            "job stayed 'queued' — the async drain never ran under `python -m backend.main` "
87	            "(the __main__/backend.main double-module entrypoint bug regressed)."
88	        )
89	    finally:
90	        proc.terminate()
91	        try:
92	            proc.wait(timeout=10)
93	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
94	            proc.kill()
95	        log.close()
96

### 149. assistant (2026-06-21T14:51:27.071Z)

"""Tests for scripts/doctor.py::init_default_config (PACKAGING_PLAN.md P0a).

doctor's --setup config write now follows ``backend.utils.app_home.default_config_path()`` — the
SAME resolver the server READS — instead of the repo root, so the two can't disagree once the app
is launched from an arbitrary CWD.
"""

import importlib.util
import os
import sys

import pytest

from backend.utils import app_home as ah

# scripts/ isn't a package — load the module by path (mirrors tests/test_nightly_regression.py).
_SCRIPT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))),
                       "scripts", "doctor.py")
_spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("doctor", _SCRIPT)
doctor = importlib.util.module_from_spec(_spec)
sys.modules["doctor"] = doctor
_spec.loader.exec_module(doctor)


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _clear_app_home_env(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, raising=False)


def test_init_writes_config_under_app_home(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, str(tmp_path))
    doctor.init_default_config()
    written = tmp_path / "config.json"
    assert written.exists(), "config.json must land under RALLY_APP_HOME (the server's read path)"
    # And it is the SAME path the loader resolves — the footgun this PR closes.
    assert written == ah.default_config_path()


def test_init_skips_when_config_already_exists(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, str(tmp_path))
    existing = tmp_path / "config.json"
    existing.write_text('{"sport": "tennis"}', encoding="utf-8")
    doctor.init_default_config()  # must NOT overwrite
    assert existing.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == '{"sport": "tennis"}'


def test_init_unset_writes_to_cwd(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    # env unset → CWD/config.json (the server, run from the same CWD, reads exactly this).
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    doctor.init_default_config()
    assert (tmp_path / "config.json").exists()


def test_init_is_noop_when_backend_import_fails(monkeypatch, tmp_path, capsys):
    monkeypatch.setenv(ah.APP_HOME_ENV, str(tmp_path))

    real_import = __builtins__["__import__"] if isinstance(__builtins__, dict) else __builtins__.__import__

    def _boom(name, *args, **kwargs):
        if name == "backend.config" or name.startswith("backend.config"):
            raise ImportError("simulated missing backend.config")
        return real_import(name, *args, **kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setattr("builtins.__import__", _boom)
    doctor.init_default_config()  # must degrade to a no-op, never raise
    assert not (tmp_path / "config.json").exists()
    assert "Falling back to no-op" in capsys.readouterr().out

### 183. assistant (2026-06-21T15:01:56.993Z)

"""Redact secret-shaped values before they leave the process (PACKAGING_PLAN.md P0b).

``GET /api/config`` returns the whole ``AppConfig`` unauthenticated. Today AppConfig stores NO
secrets by design (the Gemini key lives in the environment; ``cf_access_aud`` is a public
identifier), so this is DEFENSIVE: if a credential-shaped field is ever added to the config, this
masks it in the response and the accompanying test fails loudly rather than leaking it to any
localhost process/browser tab.

The match list is deliberately NARROW so it never masks legitimate non-secret fields such as
``edge_auth_enabled`` (a boolean), ``cf_access_aud`` (a public id), or an ML ``max_tokens`` knob.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Any

#: Field-name patterns whose VALUE is masked. Narrow on purpose (see module docstring).
SECRET_KEY_RE = re.compile(
    r"(password|passwd|secret|credential|api[_-]?key|access[_-]?token|auth[_-]?token|refresh[_-]?token)",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)

REDACTED = "***REDACTED***"


def redact_secrets(obj: Any) -> Any:
    """Return a deep copy of ``obj`` with any value under a secret-shaped KEY replaced by
    :data:`REDACTED`. Recurses into dicts and lists; scalars and non-secret keys pass through.

    A secret-named key masks its WHOLE value (even a nested dict/list — e.g. a ``credentials``
    block), so a structured secret can't slip through via recursion.
    """
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        out: dict[Any, Any] = {}
        for key, value in obj.items():
            if isinstance(key, str) and SECRET_KEY_RE.search(key):
                out[key] = REDACTED
            else:
                out[key] = redact_secrets(value)
        return out
    if isinstance(obj, (list, tuple)):
        return [redact_secrets(item) for item in obj]
    return obj

### 192. assistant (2026-06-21T15:02:32.530Z)

"""Tests for the secret-redaction helper (PACKAGING_PLAN.md P0b)."""

from backend.utils.redact import REDACTED, redact_secrets


def test_masks_common_secret_field_names():
    src = {
        "gemini_api_key": "AIza-xxx",
        "api_key": "sk-xxx",
        "access_token": "tok",
        "auth_token": "tok2",
        "refresh_token": "tok3",
        "client_secret": "shh",
        "password": "hunter2",
        "passwd": "hunter2",
        "db_credential": "pg://u:p@h",
    }
    out = redact_secrets(src)
    assert all(v == REDACTED for v in out.values()), out


def test_does_not_mask_legitimate_non_secret_fields():
    # The narrow matcher must NOT mask these real config fields / knobs.
    src = {
        "edge_auth_enabled": True,
        "cf_access_aud": "my-app-aud",
        "max_tokens": 1024,
        "sport": "badminton",
        "output_dir": "output",
        "tokenizer_name": "bert",  # 'token' substring but not an auth token field
    }
    assert redact_secrets(src) == src


def test_recurses_into_nested_dicts_and_lists():
    src = {"storage": {"gcs": {"api_key": "k", "bucket": "b"}}, "items": [{"secret": "s", "n": 1}]}
    out = redact_secrets(src)
    assert out["storage"]["gcs"]["api_key"] == REDACTED
    assert out["storage"]["gcs"]["bucket"] == "b"
    assert out["items"][0]["secret"] == REDACTED
    assert out["items"][0]["n"] == 1


def test_secret_named_key_masks_whole_nested_value():
    # A structured secret under a secret-named key is masked wholesale (no recursion leak).
    src = {"credentials": {"user": "u", "pass": "p"}}
    assert redact_secrets(src) == {"credentials": REDACTED}


def test_scalars_and_non_dict_inputs_pass_through():
    assert redact_secrets(42) == 42
    assert redact_secrets("plain") == "plain"
    assert redact_secrets([1, 2, 3]) == [1, 2, 3]


def test_does_not_mutate_input():
    src = {"api_key": "k", "nested": {"token_access": "t"}}
    snapshot = {"api_key": "k", "nested": {"token_access": "t"}}
    redact_secrets(src)
    assert src == snapshot

### 206. user (2026-06-21T15:10:58.949Z)

I have everything needed. Here are my findings.

## Findings

**[minor] backend/utils/redact.py:20 — Under-masking: several realistic secret-shaped names slip the regex.** The narrow allowlist misses `private_key`, `bearer`/`bearer_token`, `access_key`, `aws_access_key_id`, `signing_key`/`encryption_key`, `session_token`, `gh_token`, `authorization`, `cookie`. Notably `api_token` and `gemini_key` (a plausible variant of the very example the docstring/test centers on) both MISS — only `api_key`/`api-key`/`access_token` match, not `*_token` generally or `*_key` generally. This is a defensive future-proofing layer, so a miss only bites if someone later adds such a field AND it isn't caught by the accompanying test. Acceptable for P0b's stated narrow-on-purpose intent, but worth widening. Concrete fix: add alternations `|token|private[_-]?key|bearer|access[_-]?key|signing[_-]?key|encryption[_-]?key|authorization|cookie` (accepting that bare `token` would also mask `auth_token` variants and could catch a future non-secret `*token*` field — tune against the real field list).

**[nit] backend/utils/redact.py:42-43 — tuples are silently converted to lists.** `redact_secrets((1,2))` returns `[1,2]`. Irrelevant for the `/api/config` JSON path (Pydantic `model_dump(mode="json")` already emits lists), so no live impact; only matters if the helper is reused elsewhere on tuple-bearing data. Concrete fix: none required for this PR; note in the docstring that tuples normalize to lists, or `return type(obj)(...)` if tuple-preservation is ever wanted.

**No over-masking (verified):** the regex matches **zero** of the real AppConfig field names — enumerated all fields across AppConfig + ValidationConfig/relevance, TrackNet, WasbIndex, Fusion, Indexing, Output, Watermark, Stitching, Logging, Security, Storage, Deployment, Billing. In particular `cf_access_aud` (public id), `edge_auth_enabled` (bool), `auth`-adjacent names, `access_*` (e.g. none present), `*token*` (none present), `credential`/`secret` substrings (none present), and `*_key` knobs (none present) all pass through unmasked. The `cf_access_aud` field contains "access" but not "access_token", so it correctly does NOT match — no false positive.

**Serialization equivalence (verified):** `global_config.model_dump(mode="json")` is byte-equal to the old `return global_config` path (`jsonable_encoder(model)`) for every non-secret field — confirmed by direct comparison. All config fields are str/int/float/bool/list/dict/`Literal`; `Literal` values serialize as their plain string in both paths, and there are no `Path`/`datetime`/`Enum`/`set` fields that would diverge. So the response shape for real fields is unchanged.

**Test validity (verified):** `AppConfig(gemini_api_key=...)` is accepted via `model_config extra="allow"` (models.py:411), and `model_dump(mode="json")` surfaces the extra key — so the injected `gemini_api_key` is genuinely present pre-redaction and the test's `== "***REDACTED***"` assertion exercises a real masking, not a no-op.

**Leak-path analysis (clean):** A secret-named key masks its whole value before recursing (redact.py:37-38), so a structured secret (nested dict/list under e.g. `credentials`) can't escape via recursion — covered by `test_secret_named_key_masks_whole_nested_value`. Lists of dicts recurse correctly. The `None`-guard returns `{}` (main.py:166-167), leaking nothing. The one genuine residual gap is values where the secret is in the VALUE not the KEY — e.g. a connection string in a non-secret-named field like `storage.gcs = {"endpoint_url": "https://user:pass@host"}` or `input_root="gs://...@..."`. Redaction is key-name-based only, so such a value would pass through. This matches the documented design (config "stores no secrets… boto3/google auth chains supply credentials", models.py:317) and is out of scope for a key-name redactor; flagging only for completeness, not as a defect of this PR.

**Verdict: LGTM with minor follow-up** — correct, no over-masking, serialization byte-compatible, test valid; the only substantive note is the deliberately narrow regex misses some plausible future secret names (`private_key`, `bearer`, `*_token`, `access_key`), which is consistent with the PR's stated narrow-on-purpose stance but worth widening before a real credential field lands.

(Note: I did not run `git pull`/`run_all_tests.py`/ruff/mypy here — this was a scoped read-only adversarial review of the four named files; full pre-LGTM suite verification per your gate is still owed before sign-off.)

56	    """M9: CORS allow-origins from the ``RALLY_CORS_ALLOW_ORIGINS`` env var (comma-separated),
57	    default ``["*"]``. Read from the ENV (not a cwd ``config.json``) so importing ``backend.main``
58	    does zero filesystem I/O — a hosted/multi-tenant deploy sets this to lock CORS to its origin."""
